Two separately frozen, bounded full-model screens reuse the exact original checkpoint, existing proxy vocabulary rows and comparison metrics. Neither exports model weights or saves new raw logits. Each first reproduces an existing full-vocabulary coding control byte for byte before observing its three declared candidate contexts: coding, tool result and multilingual. This is calibration screening against a quantized proxy, not completed-task quality, broad equivalence or a speed result. All public pack choices and automatic profiles remain unchanged.

The affine experiment converts authenticated original expert projections eight at a time during each layer, keeping gate/up at two bits and down at three bits. Every three-bit tensor matches the authenticated earlier export's complete tensor hash. The all-three-bit coding control exactly matches the earlier full output hash. All four forwards complete in 251.402 seconds with lifetime physical peak 2,167,408,008 bytes, inside the ten-GB bound. Relative to earlier three-bit on these three cases, macro proxy KL changes from 0.591234 to 0.666165 and top-choice agreement from 0.729167 to 0.6875. Coding loses two of sixteen agreements; tool and multilingual agreement remain unchanged. This passes the explicitly frozen screening limits, while retaining the degradation as a reason to require completed tasks. It does not establish a native mixed-format or speed gain.

The lower-bit VQ experiment reuses the fully verified 2.1 artifact and the original four-bit dense source mapping. Its VQ experts, PLE, norms and unmatched tensors remain the 2.1 parent's. The existing VQ3.2/dense4 coding control matches every vocabulary output byte using the same traversal. Every destination dense class, recipe and full tensor shape is checked before replacement, and the target's own verification/runtime identities remain explicit. The four forwards complete in 105.222 seconds with lifetime physical peak 2,387,675,560 bytes, inside the unchanged ten-GB bound. Across the three fixed candidate contexts, original/mixed macro proxy KL is 0.491535/0.531509 and agreement is 0.791667/0.75. Coding agreement ties; tool and multilingual each lose one of sixteen. The separately frozen screening rule passes. The old full-VQ2.1 hold is not erased, and no similar-task-quality or throughput qualification follows.

The VQ2.1/dense4 combination is the next native screening lead because its existing weights and kernels can be reused without another large export. The metadata inspector derives a new, explicit target map; its old compiled VQ3.2 identity check rejects that new map as expected. The complete new manifest is e63df59b442df3fddb8b2fbaf68f9d1fb76e90f245fdeedddd450ad5a2697fae, composite identity bf922f0a087a0e357d527f9c31cd645aa1e96bb4ce5acc34973baed2e36752dd, bound to VQ2.1 inventory 4f63194dec2e4c3bec31289d6503cc7c886685e16e7c4aac58116d4cf0c7f037. That metadata derivation is not payload or native acceptance. The exact source, metadata and output bytes follow; native parity, representative completed tasks and useful measured performance still decide whether to integrate it.
